# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 1e-07 | 11 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst, psrr_low, psrr_high, noise_low, noise_high, line |

Defaults and seed requirements:

- nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- dc: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

- noise_low: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.
- noise_high: omitted `--cl` uses 1e-07 F. No seed accepted.

One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
`estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|

Run nominal analysis across all PVT corners at two load caps

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
pvts = ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
caps = [('7', None), ('6', 1e-6)]
for tag, cl in caps:
    for pvt in pvts:
        eid = f"c{tag}_{pvt}"
        req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
               "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
        if cl is not None:
            req["cl"] = cl
        path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
        json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
        out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        try:
            d = json.loads(out)
        except Exception:
            print(eid, "PARSE FAIL", out[:300]); continue
        if not d.get("ok"):
            print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:300]); continue
        v = {c["case_id"]: c for c in d["cases"]}
        bad = [c["case_id"] for c in d["cases"] if c.get("status") != "OK"]
        def vo(k): return v[k]["measurements"]["vout_v"] if k in v else float('nan')
        ovh = max(c["measurements"]["overhead_a"] for c in d["cases"]) * 1e6
        lh = v["light_heavy"]["measurements"]; bu = v["burst"]["measurements"]; li = v["line"]["measurements"]
        psl = v["psrr_low"]["measurements"]["psrr_db"]; psh = v["psrr_high"]["measurements"]["psrr_db"]
        nl = v["noise_low"]["measurements"]["noise"]["output_noise_rms_v"]*1e6
        nh = v["noise_high"]["measurements"]["noise"]["output_noise_rms_v"]*1e6
        rec = [w.get("recovery_s") for w in lh["recovery_windows"]]
        print(f"{eid}: vout L/M/H={vo('dc_low'):.4f}/{vo('dc_mid'):.4f}/{vo('dc_high'):.4f} ovh_max={ovh:.0f}uA "
              f"LH env=[{lh['envelope']['min_v']:.4f},{lh['envelope']['max_v']:.4f}] rec={rec} "
              f"burst env=[{bu['envelope']['min_v']:.4f},{bu['envelope']['max_v']:.4f}] "
              f"line env=[{li['envelope']['min_v']:.4f},{li['envelope']['max_v']:.4f}] "
              f"psrrL={psl['1000']:.1f}/{psl['1000000']:.1f} psrrH={psh['1000']:.1f}/{psh['1000000']:.1f} "
              f"noise={nl:.1f}/{nh:.1f}uV bad={bad}")
EOF

import json, subprocess
def run(eid, analysis, pvt):
    req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
           "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
    json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
    out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
    d = json.loads(out)
    for c in d["cases"]:
        m = c["measurements"]
        print(eid, analysis, pvt, c["status"], "vout=%.4f" % m["vout_v"], "ovh=%.0fuA" % (m["overhead_a"]*1e6),
              "psrr=", {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m.get("psrr_db", {}).items()}, "op_regulating=", m.get("op_regulating"))

Run nominal sweep over nine corners at two cap values with checks

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
pvts = ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
for tag, cl in [('a', None), ('b', 1e-6)]:
    for pvt in pvts:
        eid = f"v6{tag}_{pvt}"
        req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
               "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
        if cl is not None:
            req["cl"] = cl
        path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
        json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
        out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
        try:
            d = json.loads(out)
        except Exception:
            print(eid, "PARSE FAIL", out[:300]); continue
        if not d.get("ok"):
            print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:300]); continue
        m = {c["case_id"]: c["measurements"] for c in d["cases"]}
        fails = [f"{c['case_id']}:status" for c in d["cases"] if c["status"] != "OK"]
        for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']:
            if abs(m[k]['vout_v'] - 1.2) > 0.012: fails.append(f"{k}:dc")
        if m['dc_low']['overhead_a'] > 1e-4: fails.append("light_ovh")
        ovh_max = max(m[k]['overhead_a'] for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high'])
        if ovh_max > 3e-4: fails.append("dc_ovh")
        vref_max = max(abs(c['measurements'].get('vref_abs_a', 0)) for c in d['cases'])
        if vref_max > 1e-6: fails.append("vref")
        env_txt = {}
        for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst']:
            env = m[k].get('envelope')
            if env is None:
                fails.append(f"{k}:noenv"); continue
            env_txt[k] = (round(env['min_v'], 4), round(env['max_v'], 4))
            if env['min_v'] < 1.152 or env['max_v'] > 1.248: fails.append(f"{k}:env")
            for w in m[k].get('recovery_windows', []):
                r = w.get('recovery_s')
                if r is None or r > 2e-5: fails.append(f"{k}:recov")
        le = m['line']['envelope']
        if le['min_v'] < 1.176 or le['max_v'] > 1.224: fails.append("line")
        ps = {k: m[k]['psrr_db'] for k in ['psrr_low', 'psrr_high']}
        for k in ps:
            if ps[k]['1000'] < 40: fails.append(f"{k}:1k")
            if ps[k]['1000000'] < 30: fails.append(f"{k}:1M")
        nz = {k: m[k]['noise']['output_noise_rms_v'] * 1e6 for k in ['noise_low', 'noise_high']}
        for k in nz:
            if nz[k] > 30: fails.append(f"{k}:noise")
        print(f"{eid}: vout={m['dc_low']['vout_v']:.4f}/{m['dc_mid']['vout_v']:.4f}/{m['dc_high']['vout_v']:.4f} "
              f"ovh={ovh_max*1e6:.0f}uA env={env_txt.get('light_heavy')} "
              f"psrrL={ps['psrr_low']['1000']:.1f}/{ps['psrr_low']['1000000']:.1f} "
              f"psrrH={ps['psrr_high']['1000']:.1f}/{ps['psrr_high']['1000000']:.1f} "
              f"noise={nz['noise_low']:.1f}/{nz['noise_high']:.1f} FAILS={fails}")
EOF

Run quick FLH PSRR, DC and noise checks on the lengthened pair

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
def run(eid, analysis, pvt):
    req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
           "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
    json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
    out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
    d = json.loads(out)
    if not d.get("ok"):
        print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:400]); return
    for c in d["cases"]:
        m = c["measurements"]
        extra = {}
        if "psrr_db" in m: extra["psrr"] = {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m["psrr_db"].items()}
        if "noise" in m: extra["noise_uV"] = round(m["noise"]["output_noise_rms_v"] * 1e6, 2)
        print(eid, analysis, pvt, c["status"], "vout=%.4f" % m["vout_v"], "ovh=%.0fuA" % (m["overhead_a"] * 1e6),
              "reg=", m.get("op_regulating"), extra)
run("e1", "psrr_low", "FLH")
run("e2", "dc_low", "N")
run("e3", "dc_low", "FLH")
run("e4", "noise_low", "N")
EOF

Run FLH PSRR, DC and noise checks with the narrower PMOS mirror

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
def run(eid, analysis, pvt):
    req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
           "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
    json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
    out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
    d = json.loads(out)
    if not d.get("ok"):
        print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:400]); return
    for c in d["cases"]:
        m = c["measurements"]
        extra = {}
        if "psrr_db" in m: extra["psrr"] = {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in m["psrr_db"].items()}
        if "noise" in m: extra["noise_uV"] = round(m["noise"]["output_noise_rms_v"] * 1e6, 2)
        print(eid, analysis, pvt, c["status"], "vout=%.4f" % m["vout_v"], "ovh=%.0fuA" % (m["overhead_a"] * 1e6),
              "reg=", m.get("op_regulating"), extra)
run("e14", "psrr_low", "FLH")
run("e15", "dc_low", "N")

import json, subprocess
def run(eid, analysis, pvt):
    req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
           "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": analysis, "pvt": pvt}
    path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
    json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
    out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
    d = json.loads(out)
    if not d.get("ok"):
        print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:400]); return
    for c in d["cases"]:
        m = c["measurements"]
        extra = {}

        if "noise" in m: extra["noise_uV"] = round(m["noise"]["output_noise_rms_v"] * 1e6, 2)
        print(eid, analysis, pvt, c["status"], "vout=%.4f" % m["vout_v"], "ovh=%.0fuA" % (m["overhead_a"] * 1e6),
              "reg=", m.get("op_regulating"), extra)

Run full nominal sweep over nine corners at two cap values with checks

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
pvts = ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH']
for tag, cl in [('a', None), ('b', 1e-6)]:
    for pvt in pvts:
        eid = f"v7{tag}_{pvt}"
        req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
               "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt}
        if cl is not None:
            req["cl"] = cl
        path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
        json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
        out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
        open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
        try:
            d = json.loads(out)
        except Exception:
            print(eid, "PARSE FAIL", out[:300]); continue
        if not d.get("ok"):
            print(eid, "NOT OK", str(d)[:300]); continue
        m = {c["case_id"]: c["measurements"] for c in d["cases"]}
        fails = [f"{c['case_id']}:status" for c in d["cases"] if c["status"] != "OK"]
        for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']:
            if abs(m[k]['vout_v'] - 1.2) > 0.012: fails.append(f"{k}:dc")
        if m['dc_low']['overhead_a'] > 1e-4: fails.append("light_ovh")
        ovh_max = max(m[k]['overhead_a'] for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high'])
        if ovh_max > 3e-4: fails.append("dc_ovh")
        vref_max = max(abs(c['measurements'].get('vref_abs_a', 0)) for c in d['cases'])
        if vref_max > 1e-6: fails.append("vref")
        env_txt = {}
        for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst']:
            env = m[k].get('envelope')
            if env is None:
                fails.append(f"{k}:noenv"); continue
            env_txt[k] = (round(env['min_v'], 4), round(env['max_v'], 4))
            if env['min_v'] < 1.152 or env['max_v'] > 1.248: fails.append(f"{k}:env")
            for w in m[k].get('recovery_windows', []):
                r = w.get('recovery_s')
                if r is None or r > 2e-5: fails.append(f"{k}:recov")
        le = m['line']['envelope']
        if le['min_v'] < 1.176 or le['max_v'] > 1.224: fails.append("line")
        ps = {k: m[k]['psrr_db'] for k in ['psrr_low', 'psrr_high']}
        for k in ps:
            if not m[k].get('op_regulating', True): fails.append(f"{k}:notreg")
            if ps[k]['1000'] < 40: fails.append(f"{k}:1k")
            if ps[k]['1000000'] < 30: fails.append(f"{k}:1M")
        nz = {k: m[k]['noise']['output_noise_rms_v'] * 1e6 for k in ['noise_low', 'noise_high']}
        for k in nz:
            if nz[k] > 30: fails.append(f"{k}:noise")
        print(f"{eid}: vout={m['dc_low']['vout_v']:.4f}/{m['dc_mid']['vout_v']:.4f}/{m['dc_high']['vout_v']:.4f} "
              f"ovh={ovh_max*1e6:.0f}uA env={env_txt.get('light_heavy')} "
              f"psrrL={ps['psrr_low']['1000']:.1f}/{ps['psrr_low']['1000000']:.1f} "
              f"psrrH={ps['psrr_high']['1000']:.1f}/{ps['psrr_high']['1000000']:.1f} "
              f"noise={nz['noise_low']:.1f}/{nz['noise_high']:.1f} FAILS={fails}")
EOF

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

## Public measurement options

| Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

Check recovery windows, KCL residuals and initial hold in v8 outputs

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, glob
def fl(x):
    if isinstance(x, bool): return
    if isinstance(x, (int, float)): yield float(x)
    elif isinstance(x, dict):
        for v in x.values(): yield from fl(v)
    elif isinstance(x, list):
        for v in x: yield from fl(v)
bad = []
hold = []
for p in sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/v8a_*.json')) + sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/v8b_*.json')):
    d = json.load(open(p))
    if not d.get('ok') or d.get('fine') is not True:
        bad.append((p, 'top', d.get('ok'), d.get('fine'), d.get('category')))
    M = {c['case_id']: c['measurements'] for c in d['cases']}
    for c in d['cases']:
        if c['status'] != 'OK':
            bad.append((p, c['case_id'], c['status']))
    for cid, m in M.items():
        if 'kcl_residual_a' in m:
            mx = max(abs(x) for x in fl(m['kcl_residual_a']))
            if mx > 1e-9:
                bad.append((p, cid, 'kcl', mx))
    for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst', 'line']:
        for w in M[k].get('recovery_windows', []):
            r = w.get('recovery_s')
            if r is None or r > 2e-5:
                bad.append((p, k, 'recovery', r))
        ih = M[k].get('initial_hold')
        if ih is not None:
            hold.append((p.split('/')[-1][:-5], k, ih))
print('bad:', bad, 'count', len(bad))
d = json.load(open('/tmp/v8a_FLH.json'))
print('top:', {k: d.get(k) for k in ('ok', 'fine', 'category', 'returncode', 'timed_out')})
M = {c['case_id']: c['measurements'] for c in d['cases']}
print('initial_hold light (v8a_FLH):', M['light_heavy'].get('initial_hold'))
print('initial_hold line (v8a_FLH):', M['line'].get('initial_hold'))
print('line windows (v8a_FLH):', [(w.get('recovery_s'), round(w.get('min_v'), 5), round(w.get('max_v'), 5)) for w in M['line'].get('recovery_windows', [])])
print('hold entries:', len(hold))
EOF

atb-client help 2>&1 | grep -n -i -E "fine|category" | head -10; echo "=== 300 nF sweep ==="; python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, subprocess
pvts = ['N', 'SLC', 'SLH', 'SHC', 'SHH', 'FLC', 'FLH', 'FHC', 'FHH']
def fl(x):
    if isinstance(x, bool): return
    if isinstance(x, (int, float)): yield float(x)
    elif isinstance(x, dict):
        for v in x.values(): yield from fl(v)
    elif isinstance(x, list):
        for v in x: yield from fl(v)
def run(eid, pvt, cl):
    req = {"task_id": "LDO-QUIET-SKY130", "design_format": "netlist", "design_file": "dut.spice",
           "action": "measure", "experiment_id": eid, "analysis": "nominal", "pvt": pvt, "cl": cl}
    path = f"/tmp/req_{eid}.json"
    json.dump(req, open(path, "w"))
    out = subprocess.run(["atb-client", "--request", path], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    open(f"/tmp/{eid}.json", "w").write(out)
    return out
def check(d):
    fails = []
    M = {c['case_id']: c['measurements'] for c in d['cases']}
    for c in d['cases']:
        if c['status'] != 'OK': fails.append(f"{c['case_id']}:status")
    for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']:
        if abs(M[k]['vout_v'] - 1.2) > 0.012: fails.append(f"{k}:dc")
    if M['dc_low']['overhead_a'] > 1e-4: fails.append('light_ovh')
    if max(M[k]['overhead_a'] for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']) > 3e-4: fails.append('dc_ovh')
    for cid, m in M.items():
        if abs(m.get('vref_abs_a', 0.0)) > 1e-6: fails.append(f"{cid}:vref")
        if 'kcl_residual_a' in m and max(abs(x) for x in fl(m['kcl_residual_a'])) > 1e-9:
            fails.append(f"{cid}:kcl")
    for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst']:
        env = M[k]['envelope']
        if env['min_v'] < 1.152 or env['max_v'] > 1.248: fails.append(f"{k}:env")
    le = M['line']['envelope']
    if le['min_v'] < 1.176 or le['max_v'] > 1.224: fails.append('line:env')
    for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst', 'line']:
        for w in M[k].get('recovery_windows', []):
            if w.get('recovery_s') is None or w['recovery_s'] > 2e-5: fails.append(f"{k}:recov")
        ih = M[k].get('initial_hold')
        if ih is None or max(abs(ih['min_v'] - 1.2), abs(ih['max_v'] - 1.2)) > 0.012: fails.append(f"{k}:hold")
    for k in ['psrr_low', 'psrr_high']:
        if not M[k]['op_regulating']: fails.append(f"{k}:op")
        if M[k]['psrr_db']['1000'] < 40: fails.append(f"{k}:1k")
        if M[k]['psrr_db']['1000000'] < 30: fails.append(f"{k}:1M")
    for k in ['noise_low', 'noise_high']:
        if M[k]['noise']['output_noise_rms_v'] > 3e-5: fails.append(f"{k}:noise")
    return M, fails
for pvt in pvts:
    eid = f"v8c_{pvt}"
    out = run(eid, pvt, 3e-7)
    try:
        d = json.loads(out)
    except Exception:
        print(eid, 'PARSE FAIL', out[:300]); continue
    if not d.get('ok'):
        print(eid, 'NOT OK', str(d)[:300]); continue
    M, fails = check(d)
    print(f"{eid}: vout={M['dc_low']['vout_v']:.4f}/{M['dc_mid']['vout_v']:.4f}/{M['dc_high']['vout_v']:.4f} "
          f"ovh={max(M[k]['overhead_a'] for k in ['dc_low','dc_mid','dc_high'])*1e6:.0f}uA "
          f"env_lh={M['light_heavy']['envelope']['min_v']:.4f}/{M['light_heavy']['envelope']['max_v']:.4f} "
          f"psrrL={M['psrr_low']['psrr_db']['1000']:.1f}/{M['psrr_low']['psrr_db']['1000000']:.1f} "
          f"psrrH={M['psrr_high']['psrr_db']['1000']:.1f}/{M['psrr_high']['psrr_db']['1000000']:.1f} "
          f"noise={M['noise_low']['noise']['output_noise_rms_v']*1e6:.1f}/{M['noise_high']['noise']['output_noise_rms_v']*1e6:.1f} FAILS={fails}")
EOF

Compute worst-case margins across all v8 runs

python3 - <<'EOF'
import json, glob
def fl(x):
    if isinstance(x, bool): return
    if isinstance(x, (int, float)): yield float(x)
    elif isinstance(x, dict):
        for v in x.values(): yield from fl(v)
    elif isinstance(x, list):
        for v in x: yield from fl(v)
files = sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/v8a_*.json')) + sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/v8b_*.json'))
dc, ovl, ovd, hold, kcl, vref, rec, noise = [], [], [], [], [], [], [], []
env = {k: [] for k in ['light_heavy', 'mid_heavy', 'burst', 'line']}
ps = {k: [] for k in ['psrr_low', 'psrr_high']}
for p in files:
    d = json.load(open(p))
    M = {c['case_id']: c['measurements'] for c in d['cases']}
    dc += [M[k]['vout_v'] for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']]
    ovl.append(M['dc_low']['overhead_a'])
    ovd.append(max(M[k]['overhead_a'] for k in ['dc_low', 'dc_mid', 'dc_high']))
    for k in env:
        e = M[k]['envelope']; env[k] += [e['min_v'], e['max_v']]
        hold += [M[k]['initial_hold']['min_v'], M[k]['initial_hold']['max_v']]
        rec += [w['recovery_s'] for w in M[k].get('recovery_windows', [])]
    for k in ps:
        ps[k].append((M[k]['psrr_db']['1000'], M[k]['psrr_db']['1000000']))
    noise += [M['noise_low']['noise']['output_noise_rms_v'], M['noise_high']['noise']['output_noise_rms_v']]
    for cid, m in M.items():
        if 'kcl_residual_a' in m: kcl.append(max(abs(x) for x in fl(m['kcl_residual_a'])))
        vref.append(abs(m.get('vref_abs_a', 0.0)))
print('runs:', len(files), 'cases:', sum(len(json.load(open(p))['cases']) for p in files))
print('DC vout dev mV: min %.2f max %.2f' % ((min(dc) - 1.2) * 1e3, (max(dc) - 1.2) * 1e3))
print('initial hold dev mV: min %.2f max %.2f' % ((min(hold) - 1.2) * 1e3, (max(hold) - 1.2) * 1e3))
print('light-load overhead max uA: %.1f' % (max(ovl) * 1e6), ' DC overhead max uA: %.1f' % (max(ovd) * 1e6))
for k, v in env.items():
    print('%s envelope V: min %.4f max %.4f' % (k, min(v), max(v)))
print('recovery_s max: %s, windows: %d' % (max(rec), len(rec)))
print('psrr_low (1k, 1M) min: %.1f %.1f' % (min(a for a, b in ps['psrr_low']), min(b for a, b in ps['psrr_low'])))
print('psrr_high (1k, 1M) min: %.1f %.1f' % (min(a for a, b in ps['psrr_high']), min(b for a, b in ps['psrr_high'])))
print('noise max uV: %.2f' % (max(noise) * 1e6))
print('kcl max A: %.3g' % max(kcl), ' vref max A: %.3g' % max(vref))
EOF
